- **kt-freestyle**: Structures freestyle fix-and-verify work in long-running development sessions — isolates changes in worktrees, verifies them with the area gates, and merges back safely, while the developer decides the direction. Use when the developer says "fix this", "this is broken", "check this", "verify", "run checks", "types are wrong", "build fails", "clean this up", "refactor", or any variation of "something needs fixing", or wants to explore, prototype, or iterate on code without risk to the main branch. - **ktg-memory-compress**: Context Extension Protocol (CEP) for compressing conversation context into portable carry-packets.
